- **verification**: Proves a Task against test-first acceptance criteria using fresh Evidence, deterministic required checks, four-part mismatch diagnosis, Codex review, and non-author human review. Use when writing a work order's Verification section, validating a change before completion, triaging a failed check, auditing an Evidence claim, or recording outcome before reporting. ([mochan-tk/ttt1-codex](https://github.com/mochan-tk/ttt1-codex/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/verification) · 2 copies)
- **verification**: The Agent hand for run the check before the claim. You write the done-condition first, take one bounded action per tick, and stop honestly. An agent that never stops becomes an agent that invents work to avoid idling. Use when the work touches a run, when the user says "keep going", "a task", or "the loop", or when they ask "verification", "how do I run the check before the claim", or "verification keeps failing". Also use when a run must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven. ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/verification))

- **Verification Discipline**: Separate facts, assumptions, inferences, and guesses — and never present one as another. Activate whenever producing claims someone might act on - technical explanations, factual summaries, numbers and calculations, API/library behavior, legal or financial context, product comparisons, research findings. Trigger signals: writing "definitely", "always", "the standard way", citing a number or version, or noticing you can't remember where a claim came from. Especially important for claims from training memory about anything that changes over time. - **verification-agent**: Adversarial post-completion review of non-trivial code changes before they are reported as done. Spawns a skeptical sub-agent that actively hunts for "looks-passing-but-broken" cases: tests that pass but assert nothing, code that runs but mishandles edge cases, silent failures, unhandled errors, and claims that overstate what was actually implemented. Use before reporting completion on any multi-file or logic-heavy task, especially when the agent is about to say "done" or "all tests pass". - **verification-before-completion**: Use before claiming work is complete, fixed, or passing - requires running verification commands and confirming output before any success claim and before finishing (the `finish` tool, or `complete_task` and a `transition_card` to review or done when you have board tools) ([mhersson/contextmatrix-task-skills](https://github.com/mhersson/contextmatrix-task-skills/tree/HEAD/verification-before-completion))

- **verification-before-completion**: This document establishes a non-negotiable principle: **evidence must precede any claims of completion, fixes, or success**. ([mikeyobrien/ghclip](https://github.com/mikeyobrien/ghclip/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verification-before-completion))

([milwis/claude-config](https://github.com/milwis/claude-config/tree/HEAD/plugins/milwis-coding-toolkit/skills/verification-before-completion))
- **verification-before-completion**: タスク・修正・機能を「完了した」「直った」「動いている」と言う前に、コードを読んだだけの推測ではなく、このターンで実際に関連するコマンド/テストを実行し出力を確認する。何かの完了を報告しようとするとき——Greenフェーズ、バグ修正、リファクタ、ドキュメント更新、オンボーディング・監査タスクなど——に使う。`.claude/rules/30-testing.md` にあるGreenフェーズ後の `run` スキル推奨を、あらゆる完了宣言に一般化したものである。Status: Trial（`meta/adr/ADR-0013` 参照）。 ([minowaryo/ai-driven-development-setting-files](https://github.com/minowaryo/ai-driven-development-setting-files/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verification-before-completion))
- **verification-before-completion**: Before saying a task, fix, or feature is "done," "fixed," or "working," actually run the relevant command/test in this turn and check its output rather than asserting success from reading code alone. Use whenever about to report completion of anything — a Green phase, a bug fix, a refactor, a doc update, an onboarding/audit task. Generalizes the post-Green run-skill recommendation in .claude/rules/30-testing.md to every completion claim. Status: Trial (see meta/adr/ADR-0013). ([mistakeknot/intertest](https://github.com/mistakeknot/intertest/tree/HEAD/skills/verification-before-completion))
- **verification-before-completion**: Verify before claiming completion. Use when about to claim that work is complete, fixed, passing, or working, before committing, before creating pull requests, or before reporting status to the user. Run covered verification only when the user explicitly authorizes that operation; otherwise report unverified status with the precise verification gap. Evidence before assertions always. - **verification-before-completion**: 在声称工作完成、缺陷修复、测试或构建成功，以及准备提交或创建 PR 前，将声明映射到当前工作区的新鲜证据，并如实报告失败与阻塞。 触发条件（满足任一）： 1. 准备宣布实现、修复、测试、构建或验收成功（如“已经修好”“测试全绿”“可以交付了”） 2. 准备提交改动、创建 PR 或结束任务（例如“帮我收尾”“确认后就提 PR”） 3. 需要验证用户可见路径、错误路径或生成物，或验证命令受阻时需要判定可声明范围（如“这个页面真的能用吗”“跑不了测试还能算完成吗”） 不触发（留给任务对应的领域 Skill 或普通执行流程）： - 仍在纯探索、设计或实现阶段，尚未准备作出状态结论 - 只要求编写测试而不要求判断当前交付状态 默认路由：当“完成了吗”同时可能指实现状态和验证状态时，默认进入本 Skill，分别报告“已实现”和“已验证”，不得把二者合并猜成“已通过”。 ([Vitus213/specpatrol](https://github.com/Vitus213/specpatrol/tree/HEAD/src/specpatrol/skillpack/skills/verification-before-completion))
